Authoritative F3 descriptor/provisioning protocol: any shorter sandbox example later in this section is non-authoritative. Immediately after the no-mutation preflight and before any provider create, run `rtk proxy uv run python scripts/final_gate_orchestrator.py prepare-descriptors --preflight <attemptDir>/final-gate-preflight.json --release-candidate <attemptDir>/release-candidate.json --base-index <attemptDir>/evidence-index.tasks-1-40.json --planned-state-prefix final-gate/<release-sha>/<attempt-id> --planned-intent-ledger <attemptDir>/final-gate-qa-sandbox-intent.json --planned-sandbox-receipt <attemptDir>/final-gate-qa-sandbox.json --planned-provision-outcome <attemptDir>/final-gate-F3-provision-precleanup.json --coordinator-lane F1:scripts/run_plan_compliance_audit.py --child-lane F2:lazycodex-code-reviewer --child-lane F3:lazycodex-qa-executor --child-lane F4:lazycodex-gate-reviewer --supported-fields task_name,fork_turns,message --require-powerbi-session-executor-instances 2 --require-zone-count-at-least 2 --forbid-public-session-executor-ip --require-distinct-profile-disk-generations --require-distinct-internal-endpoints --require-executor-specific-identity-audience-mtls --require-provider-readback-fields digest,region,zone,enabled,identity,audience,ingress,provider-generation,task-count,parallelism,retries,timeout,profile-disk-generation,internal-endpoint,mtls-generation --max-child-parallel 3 --require-total-agent-slots 4 --require-free-child-slots 3 --descriptor-out <attemptDir>/final-gate-F3-descriptor.json --json <attemptDir>/final-gate-launch-plan.json --freeze`. That command freezes the F3 lane descriptor, expected compensation-aware workload inventory, disjoint prefixes, receipt paths, prompt hash, and launch plan without touching a provider. Provision only afterward with exactly `rtk proxy uv run python scripts/provision_final_gate_qa_sandbox.py --f3-descriptor <attemptDir>/final-gate-F3-descriptor.json --preflight <attemptDir>/final-gate-preflight.json --release-candidate <attemptDir>/release-candidate.json --image-receipt <attemptDir>/task-39-image.json --terraform-state-ready <attemptDir>/task-6-terraform-state-ready.json --isolated-state-prefix final-gate/<release-sha>/<attempt-id> --intent-ledger <attemptDir>/final-gate-qa-sandbox-intent.json --persist-before-each-create --fixture tests/fixtures/e2e/sanitized-final-manifest.json --full-compute-overlay api,worker,dispatcher,recovery-broker,reconciler,backup,cutover-guardian,powerbi-session-broker,routing-compensation-broker,powerbi-session-executor --powerbi-session-executor-instances 2 --require-zone-count-at-least 2 --forbid-public-session-executor-ip --require-distinct-profile-disk-generations --require-distinct-internal-endpoints --require-executor-specific-identity-audience-mtls --record-provider-fields digest,region,zone,enabled,identity,audience,ingress,provider-generation,task-count,parallelism,retries,timeout,profile-disk-generation,internal-endpoint,mtls-generation --dedicated-neon-branch --dedicated-gcs-namespace --dedicated-queue --dedicated-powerbi-workspace --dedicated-qa-identities --sandbox-backup-mode disposable-no-lock --forbid-protected-buckets --forbid-production --forbid-owner-data --json <attemptDir>/final-gate-qa-sandbox.json`. Whether that command succeeds, fails, times out, is interrupted, or is killed before/between any create, the coordinator next runs `rtk proxy uv run python scripts/final_gate_orchestrator.py record-f3-provision-outcome --descriptor <attemptDir>/final-gate-F3-descriptor.json --intent-ledger-if-present <attemptDir>/final-gate-qa-sandbox-intent.json --sandbox-if-present <attemptDir>/final-gate-qa-sandbox.json --command-exit <exit-or-interrupted> --map-success-to READY_FOR_CHILD_PRE_CLEANUP --map-other-to PROVISION_FAILED_PRE_CLEANUP --out <attemptDir>/final-gate-F3-provision-precleanup.json --freeze`; a missing provision receipt is never treated as success. Only `READY_FOR_CHILD_PRE_CLEANUP` may be bound through `rtk proxy uv run python scripts/final_gate_orchestrator.py bind-f3-sandbox --descriptor <attemptDir>/final-gate-F3-descriptor.json --provision-outcome <attemptDir>/final-gate-F3-provision-precleanup.json --intent-ledger <attemptDir>/final-gate-qa-sandbox-intent.json --sandbox <attemptDir>/final-gate-qa-sandbox.json --json <attemptDir>/final-gate-F3-sandbox-binding.json --freeze` and launched as F3. Before its first create, the provisioner live-revalidates the state-ready bucket/prefix/versioning/soft-delete/CMEK policy and rejects drift. It uses the frozen digest for every workload, creates sandbox-only secrets/IAM and dispatcher/queue bindings, persists intent before each mutation and resolved IDs after each create, and never accepts Todo 40 pilot IDs. `disposable-no-lock` is production-forbidden and cannot satisfy or weaken Todo 38/F4 retention evidence.

Authoritative F3 cleanup protocol: provisioning and F3 expose only pre-cleanup outcomes. If provisioning does not reach `READY_FOR_CHILD_PRE_CLEANUP`, the coordinator runs `rtk proxy uv run python scripts/final_gate_orchestrator.py materialize-f3-provision-failure --launch-plan <attemptDir>/final-gate-launch-plan.json --f3-descriptor <attemptDir>/final-gate-F3-descriptor.json --provision-outcome <attemptDir>/final-gate-F3-provision-precleanup.json --require-status PROVISION_FAILED_PRE_CLEANUP --out <attemptDir>/final-F3-manual-qa-precleanup.md --freeze`; otherwise F3 alone writes `READY_FOR_CLEANUP`, `CHANGES_REQUESTED_PRE_CLEANUP`, or `WAITING_FOR_OWNER_PRE_CLEANUP`. None is terminal. For every outcome, missing sandbox receipt, timeout, interruption, retry, or kill before/between any create, the coordinator always runs `rtk proxy uv run python scripts/destroy_final_gate_qa_sandbox.py --f3-descriptor <attemptDir>/final-gate-F3-descriptor.json --provision-outcome <attemptDir>/final-gate-F3-provision-precleanup.json --intent-ledger-if-present <attemptDir>/final-gate-qa-sandbox-intent.json --sandbox-if-present <attemptDir>/final-gate-qa-sandbox.json --allow-partial-provisioning --require-recorded-or-descriptor-planned-only --require-zero-active-resources --require-zero-live-readable-state --allow-retained-class RETAINED_TERRAFORM_STATE_TOMBSTONE --record-retained-generation-ids-expiry-cmek-cost --require-retained-inaccessible-to-sandbox --json <attemptDir>/final-gate-qa-sandbox-cleanup.json`, then runs `rtk proxy uv run python scripts/final_gate_orchestrator.py seal-f3 --launch-plan <attemptDir>/final-gate-launch-plan.json --f3-descriptor <attemptDir>/final-gate-F3-descriptor.json --provision-outcome <attemptDir>/final-gate-F3-provision-precleanup.json --precleanup-receipt <attemptDir>/final-F3-manual-qa-precleanup.md --qa-sandbox-intent-if-present <attemptDir>/final-gate-qa-sandbox-intent.json --qa-sandbox-if-present <attemptDir>/final-gate-qa-sandbox.json --qa-sandbox-cleanup <attemptDir>/final-gate-qa-sandbox-cleanup.json --map-ready-to APPROVE --map-changes-to CHANGES_REQUESTED --map-waiting-or-provision-failure-to WAITING_FOR_OWNER --require-zero-active-resources --require-zero-live-readable-state --allow-retained-class RETAINED_TERRAFORM_STATE_TOMBSTONE --require-retained-inaccessible-to-sandbox --out <attemptDir>/final-F3-manual-qa.md --freeze`. Cleanup failure prevents every terminal seal. Retry first adopts the descriptor and intent ledger, cleans partial resources, and then creates a new attempt directory rather than overwriting frozen receipts. Terminal `APPROVE` additionally requires `READY_FOR_CHILD_PRE_CLEANUP`, the immutable sandbox binding, full overlay inventory, sandbox backup-mode receipt, complete sanitized E2E evidence, and F3 `READY_FOR_CLEANUP`; F4 alone supplies protected-retention proof.
> Runs in parallel after the post-Todo-40 release-candidate assembly checkpoint and before Todo 41 within four total agent slots: coordinator-local F1 plus three child lanes F2-F4. First run `rtk proxy uv run python scripts/final_gate_preflight.py --release-candidate <attemptDir>/release-candidate.json --base-index <attemptDir>/evidence-index.tasks-1-40.json --require-role lazycodex-code-reviewer --require-role lazycodex-qa-executor --require-role lazycodex-gate-reviewer --require-script scripts/run_plan_compliance_audit.py --require-script scripts/run_code_security_review.py --require-script scripts/run_runtime_audit.py --require-script scripts/provision_final_gate_qa_sandbox.py --require-script scripts/destroy_final_gate_qa_sandbox.py --require-browseros-neo --require-windows-receipt <owner-windows-fabric-receipt> --orchestrator scripts/final_gate_orchestrator.py --require-total-agent-slots 4 --require-free-child-slots 3 --json <attemptDir>/final-gate-preflight.json`. It validates immutable inputs, callable roles/procedures, BrowserOS, Windows/Fabric, and capacity without mutation. Then run `prepare-descriptors` and the authoritative descriptor-bound provisioning/outcome sequence before launching any child. On provisioning success, freeze the sandbox binding, launch and record F2-F4 from the predeclared descriptors, and only after all three child launch records exist start local F1; all four lanes validate the same release candidate/base index and overlap while F3 alone may mutate the isolated sandbox. On provisioning failure, launch no child and do not fabricate F3: materialize its `PROVISION_FAILED_PRE_CLEANUP` receipt from the descriptor/outcome, clean and seal it `WAITING_FOR_OWNER`; no join can approve. F1, F2, and F4 write terminal receipts. F3 writes only a declared pre-cleanup outcome, after which the coordinator executes all-exit cleanup/seal and the authoritative validator. No source commit may occur after freeze. Join requires four terminal `APPROVE` receipts; any change request requires a fixed/new release candidate and full rerun, while missing capability returns terminal `WAITING_FOR_OWNER` only after sandbox cleanup.
The F3 sandbox uses dedicated disposable sanitized QA storage that is explicitly outside every locked protected Final/backup bucket; and therefore its resource cleanup requires zero active resources rather than impossible deletion of protected backend history. The state backend may retain only the named inaccessible `RETAINED_TERRAFORM_STATE_TOMBSTONE` class with generation/expiry/CMEK/cost inventory; It tests create/read/retry semantics but never weakens or substitutes for retention evidence. F4 independently verifies the real preview protected-bucket locks read-only from Todo 6/38 receipts while F3 has no credentials for those buckets.

Child launch recording is literal and no-overwrite: after each of the three `spawn_agent` calls returns, run respectively `rtk proxy uv run python scripts/final_gate_orchestrator.py record-child --launch-plan <attemptDir>/final-gate-launch-plan.json --lane F2 --agent-id <f2-agent-id> --json <attemptDir>/final-gate-launch-F2.json`; `rtk proxy uv run python scripts/final_gate_orchestrator.py record-child --launch-plan <attemptDir>/final-gate-launch-plan.json --lane F3 --agent-id <f3-agent-id> --json <attemptDir>/final-gate-launch-F3.json`; and `rtk proxy uv run python scripts/final_gate_orchestrator.py record-child --launch-plan <attemptDir>/final-gate-launch-plan.json --lane F4 --agent-id <f4-agent-id> --json <attemptDir>/final-gate-launch-F4.json`. The coordinator must possess all three immutable launch records before starting local F1 or waiting on any child.

- [ ] F1. Plan compliance audit
  Inputs: immutable release-candidate SHA, plan SHA, frozen Todo 1-40 index path/SHA, preflight receipt, clean changed-path allowlist, and the current coordinator actor ID. After F2-F4 have all been launched and recorded, run F1 locally and concurrently as `rtk proxy uv run python scripts/run_plan_compliance_audit.py --preflight <attemptDir>/final-gate-preflight.json --release-candidate <attemptDir>/release-candidate.json --base-index <attemptDir>/evidence-index.tasks-1-40.json --plan-sha <plan-sha> --actor-id <coordinator-agent-id> --map-all-must-and-todos --forbid-supabase-duckdb-draft-bi-production --out <attemptDir>/final-F1-plan-compliance.md`; then record the immutable local lane identity with `rtk proxy uv run python scripts/final_gate_orchestrator.py record-local --launch-plan <attemptDir>/final-gate-launch-plan.json --lane F1 --actor-id <coordinator-agent-id> --procedure scripts/run_plan_compliance_audit.py --receipt <attemptDir>/final-F1-plan-compliance.md --json <attemptDir>/final-gate-launch-F1.json`. It performs no implementation. Run `rtk proxy uv run python scripts/validate_gate_receipt.py --lane F1 --receipt <attemptDir>/final-F1-plan-compliance.md --preflight <attemptDir>/final-gate-preflight.json --release-candidate <attemptDir>/release-candidate.json --base-index <attemptDir>/evidence-index.tasks-1-40.json --json <attemptDir>/final-F1-validation.json`; missing/mismatched/nonterminal inputs yield `WAITING_FOR_OWNER`/`INCONCLUSIVE`, never approval. Evidence is separate and not inserted into the base index. Blocks Todo 41.
- [ ] F2. Code quality and security review
  Inputs: the same preflight, release-candidate SHA, exact frozen Todo 1-40 index path/SHA, and committed callable procedure hashes; F2 does not depend on or consume F1 output. Invocation: call exactly `spawn_agent({"task_name":"f2_code_security","fork_turns":"none","message":"DECLARED_ROLE=lazycodex-code-reviewer. READ_ONLY F2. Validate <preflight>, <release-candidate>, and <base-index>; run committed scripts/run_code_security_review.py and scripts/run_runtime_audit.py on the literal release-candidate SHA; require separate code-quality, security, and runtime-audit receipts bound to their procedure hashes; inspect typed boundaries, dependencies, RLS/grants, hostile XLSX/path/formula handling, GCS/Cloud Tasks/Power BI scopes, create-only Final object plus local-only SQL Final transaction, outbox/fencing, migrations/IaC, tests, and live receipt hashes; aggregate only into <attemptDir>/final-F2-code-security.md; return APPROVE, CHANGES_REQUESTED, or WAITING_FOR_OWNER."})`, launch it as one of the three prepared children and immediately record its agent ID in `<attemptDir>/final-gate-launch-F2.json`. Then run `rtk proxy uv run python scripts/validate_gate_receipt.py --lane F2 --require-procedure scripts/run_code_security_review.py --require-procedure scripts/run_runtime_audit.py --require-sublanes code-quality,security,runtime-audit --receipt <attemptDir>/final-F2-code-security.md --preflight <attemptDir>/final-gate-preflight.json --release-candidate <attemptDir>/release-candidate.json --base-index <attemptDir>/evidence-index.tasks-1-40.json --json <attemptDir>/final-F2-validation.json`; every sublane must approve, while any blocking/high finding or missing receipt yields aggregate `CHANGES_REQUESTED`/`WAITING_FOR_OWNER`. No external/unpinned skill is required. Evidence remains separate. Blocks Todo 41.
- [ ] F3. Real manual QA
  Inputs: the same preflight/release-candidate/index identities, Todo 40 target-preflight, committed exhaustive operation-delta schema, immutable operation-ID-bound expected-delta receipt, frozen pre/post-pilot inventories, immutable sandbox intent ledger, complete sandbox compute/data overlay receipt, predetermined sanitized E2E/Power BI fixture, BrowserOS connection, and owner-provided Windows Power BI Desktop/Fabric runner from Todo 4. F3 is denied every Todo 40 pilot and protected-retention resource and verifies the pilot only through frozen hashes/classifications/signed receipt IDs/sanitized aggregates; it never opens, mutates, or captures owner-period data. Invoke only the authoritative F3 child payload above, record its agent ID immediately, and accept only its pre-cleanup outcomes. The coordinator then runs the authoritative all-exit cleanup, seal, validator, and Todo 1 redaction scanner. A sealed `APPROVE` requires the frozen digest on the complete compensation-aware isolated workload set, exactly two no-public-IP session executors in distinct zones with executor-specific identity/audience/mTLS, distinct encrypted profile-disk generations/internal endpoints, complete provider readback, disjoint state/resource identities, disposable-no-lock backup/restore evidence that makes no retention claim, sanitized browser/worker/GCS/Neon/Cloud Tasks/Power BI Service five-page evidence, exact reconciliation of every schema category against the expected operation ID, equality of the frozen post-pilot inventory before versus after F3, terminal/absent temporary resources, no unlisted delta, zero active resources/live-readable sandbox state, and no remnant except the exact inaccessible `RETAINED_TERRAFORM_STATE_TOMBSTONE` inventory created by the run and bound to generation IDs, expiry, CMEK and cost before the terminal seal. `CHANGES_REQUESTED`/`WAITING_FOR_OWNER` also cannot become terminal until cleanup succeeds. Neither artifact enters the base index. Blocks Todo 41.
- [ ] F4. Scope fidelity and operations gate
  Inputs: the same preflight/release-candidate/index identities, workspace-authority manifest, Todo 40 target-preflight, committed exhaustive operation-delta schema, immutable operation-ID-bound expected-delta receipt, frozen pre/post-pilot provider inventories, cost/restore/runbook/license evidence, and production fingerprints proving no mutation. F4 has read-only credentials for the pilot inventory and no access to F3's sandbox. Invocation: call exactly `spawn_agent({"task_name":"f4_scope_ops","fork_turns":"none","message":"DECLARED_ROLE=lazycodex-gate-reviewer. READ_ONLY F4. Validate <preflight>, <release-candidate>, <base-index>, and workspace authority; run scripts/psi_workspace_guard.py verify and scripts/validate_evidence_index.py; refuse <qa-sandbox> resource IDs; reconcile changed paths, the immutable Todo 40 target-preflight, exhaustive delta schema, operation-ID-bound expected receipt, and exact pre/post Neon/GCS/GCP/Power BI/Cloud Tasks inventories, cost, backup/restore, provider retention locks, licenses, production fingerprints, and documented architecture without mutation; write only <attemptDir>/final-F4-scope-ops.md; return APPROVE, CHANGES_REQUESTED, or WAITING_FOR_OWNER."})`, launch it as one of the three prepared children and immediately record its agent ID in `<attemptDir>/final-gate-launch-F4.json`. Then run `rtk proxy uv run python scripts/validate_gate_receipt.py --lane F4 --pilot-read-only --forbid-sandbox <attemptDir>/final-gate-qa-sandbox.json --pilot-target-preflight <attemptDir>/task-40-target-preflight.json --pilot-before <attemptDir>/task-40-pilot-before.json --pilot-delta-schema policy/pilot/operation-delta-schema.json --pilot-expected-delta <attemptDir>/task-40-expected-delta.json --pilot-after <attemptDir>/task-40-pilot-after.json --require-exact-operation-bound-pilot-deltas --require-all-schema-categories --require-temporary-terminal-or-absent --forbid-unlisted-delta --require-pilot-unchanged-during-f4 --receipt <attemptDir>/final-F4-scope-ops.md --preflight <attemptDir>/final-gate-preflight.json --release-candidate <attemptDir>/release-candidate.json --base-index <attemptDir>/evidence-index.tasks-1-40.json --json <attemptDir>/final-F4-validation.json`; `APPROVE` requires exact reconciliation with no pilot mutation, sandbox access, user/unrelated files, secrets, raw data, production mutation, or undocumented architecture. Evidence remains separate. Blocks Todo 41.

After F1, F2, and F4 are terminal and F3 has been sealed terminal only after its cleanup receipt proves zero active sandbox resources/live-readable state and only the exact allowlisted inaccessible retained-state tombstone inventory, run exactly `rtk proxy uv run python scripts/final_gate_orchestrator.py join --launch-plan <attemptDir>/final-gate-launch-plan.json --launch-record <attemptDir>/final-gate-launch-F1.json --launch-record <attemptDir>/final-gate-launch-F2.json --launch-record <attemptDir>/final-gate-launch-F3.json --launch-record <attemptDir>/final-gate-launch-F4.json --qa-sandbox <attemptDir>/final-gate-qa-sandbox.json --qa-sandbox-cleanup <attemptDir>/final-gate-qa-sandbox-cleanup.json --receipt <attemptDir>/final-F1-plan-compliance.md --receipt <attemptDir>/final-F2-code-security.md --receipt <attemptDir>/final-F3-manual-qa.md --receipt <attemptDir>/final-F4-scope-ops.md --validation-receipt <attemptDir>/final-F1-validation.json --validation-receipt <attemptDir>/final-F2-validation.json --validation-receipt <attemptDir>/final-F3-validation.json --validation-receipt <attemptDir>/final-F4-validation.json --require-all-validation-receipts-approve --require-validation-receipt-sha-matches-lane --require-terminal --require-distinct-lane-actor-prompt-or-procedure --require-role-lane-map --require-total-agent-slots 4 --require-child-launches 3 --require-pilot-untouched --require-zero-active-sandbox-resources --require-zero-live-readable-sandbox-state --allow-retained-class RETAINED_TERRAFORM_STATE_TOMBSTONE --require-retained-inaccessible-to-sandbox --json <attemptDir>/final-gate-join.json`; it validates the coordinator-local F1 procedure, three supported-field child descriptors, declared roles, four actor IDs, prompt/procedure/input hashes, terminal states, cleanup, and four-total-slot/three-child capacity without editing the base index. Then run exactly `rtk proxy uv run python scripts/build_final_gate_bundle.py --base-index <attemptDir>/evidence-index.tasks-1-40.json --join-receipt <attemptDir>/final-gate-join.json --receipt <attemptDir>/final-F1-plan-compliance.md --receipt <attemptDir>/final-F2-code-security.md --receipt <attemptDir>/final-F3-manual-qa.md --receipt <attemptDir>/final-F4-scope-ops.md --validation-receipt <attemptDir>/final-F1-validation.json --validation-receipt <attemptDir>/final-F2-validation.json --validation-receipt <attemptDir>/final-F3-validation.json --validation-receipt <attemptDir>/final-F4-validation.json --require-all-validation-receipts-approve --release-candidate-sha <sha> --out <attemptDir>/final-gates.json --freeze`, then `rtk proxy uv run python scripts/validate_final_gate_bundle.py --bundle <attemptDir>/final-gates.json --base-index <attemptDir>/evidence-index.tasks-1-40.json --join-receipt <attemptDir>/final-gate-join.json --receipt <attemptDir>/final-F1-plan-compliance.md --receipt <attemptDir>/final-F2-code-security.md --receipt <attemptDir>/final-F3-manual-qa.md --receipt <attemptDir>/final-F4-scope-ops.md --validation-receipt <attemptDir>/final-F1-validation.json --validation-receipt <attemptDir>/final-F2-validation.json --validation-receipt <attemptDir>/final-F3-validation.json --validation-receipt <attemptDir>/final-F4-validation.json --require-all-validation-receipts-approve --release-candidate-sha <sha> --require-gates F1,F2,F3,F4`. The join, builder, and validator hash-bind all four lane-validation receipts; the builder accepts only four distinct `APPROVE` lane receipts whose matching validation receipts also approve bound to the same immutable base-index SHA and release-candidate SHA, verifies every referenced artifact, the F3 READY_FOR_CLEANUP pre-receipt, cleanup-before-seal ordering, and cleanup SHA, writes atomically without following symlinks, refuses overwrite, and emits its own checksum. Todo 41 cannot begin without this validated bundle.

## Commit strategy

- Use one atomic conventional source commit per todo after its non-provider unit/static gate passes; implementation and boundary tests stay together. Every task whose acceptance consumes a container, BrowserOS, Neon, GCS, GCP, Cloud Tasks, Power BI, or backup/restore surface (including 6, 12-15, 18-20, 23, 25-30, and 31-40) then runs that surface from the exact clean committed SHA and does not expose it downstream until the receipt passes. If the post-commit surface fails, make a narrowly scoped same-todo fix commit, rebuild/retest the new SHA, invalidate the failed receipt, and record only the latest passing task SHA; never amend or reuse the failed image/provider receipt. Do not batch unrelated waves or commit generated/raw workbooks, secrets, provider state, attempt-directory evidence, `.env`, `.neon` credentials, or user files. Todo 40 follows the explicit pilot-receipt -> exact-two-path documentation-only commit -> release-candidate/index-freeze ordering above and proves that commit leaves every Todo 39 tested execution-input hash, not only the deployed image context, unchanged; the aggregate index is post-commit evidence, not a commit input.
- Before each Todo 1-40 source commit run its declared non-provider gate and `rtk git diff --check`; stage explicit paths only. After the commit, rerun the task authoritative QA commands from a clean checkout or use its commit-bound provider/container wrapper; only that post-commit run may write the indexed task receipt. Precommit green output is development feedback, not evidence. For task-specific committed-image QA, `build_local_test_image.py` must bind the task SHA/context before the task can complete. After the Todo 40 commit, freeze the current Git SHA with `finalize_release_candidate.py`, build/validate the base index, and prohibit further source commits until all F1-F4 receipts finish. Before F1-F4 also run the common gate, committed container build, preview E2E/pilot, and `rtk git status --short` allowlist check. Any code/plan/test fix creates a new commit and invalidates the release-candidate manifest, base index, and all four lane receipts; Todo 41 occurs only after a newly valid set.
- Provider/IaC commits contain declarations and sanitized IDs only. Production apply receipts and operational evidence live in the attempt evidence directory, not in source history.

## Success criteria

- Uploading a valid weekly source set and pressing Run creates one immutable, fully pinned job: exact snapshot-identity tuples and canonical run keys are reused with append-only duplicate audits, while corrected bytes, normalized metadata, explicit selection/approval/baseline/waiver, or governed-version changes create new snapshot/run identities.
- The Python/Polars engine deterministically reproduces the approved rules and 17-sheet workbook, passes deep parity/native-open/formula/package gates, and stays inside agreed container runtime/RSS limits without DuckDB/pandas/Spark or a rewrite.
- Neon Auth/RLS behind FastAPI and distinct service roles prevent anonymous, disabled, wrong-team, wrong-tenant, and over-privileged access; all provider secrets remain managed and redacted.
- A validated Draft can be reviewed and published exactly once as immutable Final bytes; cross-provider faults reconcile safely, rollback preserves history, and BI refresh failure never corrupts or reverses Final.
- Only Final data appears in the curated Neon `bi` schema and Power BI Import model; all five approved report pages render, filter, reconcile to Final totals, and show refresh freshness/failure accurately.
- Preview E2E, real weekly pilot, restore rehearsal, cost-capacity projection, four pre-production independent reviews, and then explicit owner production authorization/cutover all pass with artifact-backed evidence; no unrelated/user files or unapproved production resources change.
